# ==============================================================================
#  大炎·珍玩集册 —— 加成汇总：把已收藏珍玩「当前品阶」的真实修正累加成定点整数
#  · YAN_bonus_<key>     万分比定点整数（4762 = 47.62%）
#  · YAN_bonus_<key>_i   百分数整数位   · _d 十分位(0..9)  · _f 后两位(0..99)
#  · YAN_bonus_<key>_sgn 1 = 该项合计为负（显示符号走 YAN_Sign_<key> 链，别把 '+' 烘进文案）
#  · YAN_bonus_pp_gain_q 加法项的「百分之一/天」计数（35 → 0.35/天）
#  · 显示端用 [?变量] 直接打印，见 ARF_YAN_Collection_Bonus_l_simp_chinese.yml
#  · 挂接：YAN_Collection_Open / _Grant / _Show_Summary 末尾调用
# ==============================================================================

	round_variable = YAN_bonus_pp_gain_q
	set_variable = { YAN_bonus_pp_gain_f = YAN_bonus_pp_gain_q }
	modulo_variable = { YAN_bonus_pp_gain_f = 100 }
	set_variable = { YAN_bonus_pp_gain_i = YAN_bonus_pp_gain_q }
	subtract_from_variable = { YAN_bonus_pp_gain_i = 50 }
	divide_variable = { YAN_bonus_pp_gain_i = 100 }
	round_variable = YAN_bonus_pp_gain_i
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_pp_gain_neg = 1 } }
		set_variable = { YAN_bonus_pp_gain_neg = 0 }
		multiply_variable = { YAN_bonus_pp_gain = -1 }
	}
}
